### [1] [Dive into the Scene: Breaking the Perceptual Bottleneck in Vision-Language Decision Making via Focus Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04046)
*Boyuan Xiao,Bohong Chen,Yumeng Li,Ji Feng,Yao-Xiang Ding,Kun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneDiver是一种基于粗到精注意计划的VLM方法，通过构建全场景图理解场景，迭代分解任务，并将注意能力蒸馏到VLA中，减少视觉幻觉并保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身视觉语言决策任务中，VLMs和VLAs都存在相同的感知瓶颈：由于无法区分任务相关对象和干扰物，导致视觉幻觉。虽然直接关注关键对象看似简单，但有效聚焦需要深度场景理解。

Method: 提出SceneDiver方法：1）利用VLMs的长时规划能力，先构建整体场景图建立初步理解；2）通过识别、理解和分析的迭代循环，逐步将任务分解为更简单的子问题；3）设计轻量级适配器，将精心规划的聚焦能力蒸馏到VLAs中。

Result: 在标准具身AI基准测试中，该方法显著减少了VLMs和VLAs的视觉幻觉，同时在需要快速执行的任务中保持了计算效率。

Conclusion: SceneDiver通过粗到精的聚焦规划方法，结合场景图理解和任务分解，有效解决了VLMs和VLAs的视觉幻觉问题，并通过蒸馏技术实现了从规划模型到反应控制模型的有效知识迁移。

Abstract: In embodied vision-language decision making tasks such as robotic manipulation and navigation, Vision-Language and Vision-Language-Action Models (VLMs & VLAs) are powerful tools with different benefits: VLMs are better at long-term planning, while VLAs are better at reactive control. However, their performance is limited by the same perceptual bottleneck: visual hallucinations arise due to the models' inability to distinguish task-relevant objects from distractors. In principle, accurate identification and focus on critical objects while filtering out irrelevant ones is the key to break this limitation. A straightforward solution is one-step focus: directly attending to essential objects. However, this approach proves ineffective because effective focus inherently requires deep scene understanding. To this end, we propose SceneDiver, a coarse-to-fine focus plan generation method for VLMs leveraging their long-term planning abilities, that first constructs a holistic scene graph to establish initial comprehension, then progressively decomposes the task into simpler sub-problems through an iterative cycle of recognition, understanding, and analysis. To enable reactive control, we also design a lightweight adapter for distilling the deliberate focus ability into VLAs. Evaluations on standard embodied AI benchmarks confirm that our method substantially reduces visual hallucinations for both VLMs and VLAs, while preserving computational efficiency in tasks requiring fast execution. Our code and data are released at: https://future-item.github.io/SceneDiver.

</details>


### [2] [Weakly Supervised Incremental Segmentation via Semantic Anchors and Spatial Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04060)
*Zhonggai Wang,Kai Fang,Guangyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SASA是一种抗漂移的弱增量语义分割方法，通过语义锚点和空间仲裁机制稳定语义学习，有效缓解弱监督下的特征漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱增量语义分割在持续引入噪声监督时面临严重问题：类级别表示逐渐被破坏，导致特征漂移和语义损坏，新学习的类会覆盖旧的类。

Method: 提出SASA方法，包含两个核心机制：1)表示层面：引入可学习token的语义锚点作为刚性类级别参考来保持长期语义一致性，配合弹性残差适应实现受控的实例特定细化；2)监督层面：开发空间标签仲裁机制，执行几何感知决策来直接过滤不可靠信号并强制执行严格的"一个对象一个类别"约束。

Result: 在标准基准测试上的大量实验表明，SASA方法始终优于现有的最先进方法，特别是在具有挑战性的多步增量设置中。

Conclusion: 通过协同稳定表示和改善监督可靠性，SASA有效缓解了弱监督下的特征漂移问题。该方法为弱增量语义分割提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Weakly Incremental Learning for Semantic Segmentation (WILSS) suffers from the continuous introduction of noisy supervision, which progressively corrupts class-level representations, leading to severe feature drift and semantic corruption, thereby causing newly learned classes to overwrite old ones. To address these issues, we propose a drift-resilient WILSS approach, named SASA, designed to stabilize semantic learning via Semantic Anchors and Spatial Arbitration. Specifically, at the representation level, we introduce semantic anchors of learnable tokens as rigid class-level references to preserve long-term semantic identity. Complementary to this, an elastic residual adaptation facilitates controlled, instance-specific refinement, ensuring a stable yet flexible learning trajectory. At the supervision level, we develop a Spatial Label Arbitration mechanism that performs geometry-aware decisions to directly filter unreliable signals and enforce a strict "one object, one class" constraint. By synergistically stabilizing representations and improving supervision reliability, SASA effectively mitigates feature drift under weak supervision. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, particularly in challenging multi-step incremental settings. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhonggaiWang/SASA.

</details>


### [3] [Intra-Modal Neighbors Never Lie: Rectifying Inter-Modal Noisy Correspondence via Graph-Based Intra-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04061)
*Yang Liu,Wentao Feng,Shu-Dong Huang,Yalan Ye,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IN2R是一种新的跨模态检索框架，通过合成连续的软原型来修正噪声对应关系，替代传统的离散标签选择方法，在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模网络采集的数据集存在噪声对应问题，严重降低模型泛化能力。现有方法主要采用"离散选择"范式，存在单点脆弱性和离散化误差。需要从寻找替代标签转向合成可靠监督目标的新范式。

Method: 提出IN2R框架：利用模态内数据的几何稳定性，通过图精炼器对从动态跨模态记忆检索的邻居进行关系推理，合成反映局部语义邻域共识的连续软原型，而非传播离散标签。

Result: 在Flickr30K、MS-COCO和CC152K数据集上的广泛实验表明，IN2R显著优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: IN2R通过从离散标签选择转向合成连续软原型的新范式，有效修正跨模态不对齐问题，为解决噪声对应问题提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale web-harvested datasets have fueled the progress of cross-modal retrieval but inevitably suffer from noisy correspondence, which severely degrades model generalization. Existing methods primarily address this by filtering out noise or seeking a substitute label, yet they predominantly remain bound by a "Discrete Selection" paradigm. We argue that relying on a single discrete proxy induces Single-Point Fragility and Discretization Error. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel framework, Intra-modal Neighbor-aware Noise Rectification (IN2R), which shifts the paradigm from searching for a substitute to synthesizing a reliable supervision target. Leveraging the intrinsic geometric stability of intra-modal data, IN2R employs a Graph Refiner to perform relational reasoning over neighbors retrieved from a dynamic Cross-Model Memory. Instead of propagating discrete labels, our method synthesizes a continuous, soft prototype that reflects the consensus of the local semantic neighborhood, effectively rectifying inter-modal misalignment. Extensive experiments on Flickr30K, MS-COCO, and CC152K demonstrate that IN2R significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/liuyyy111/IN2R.

</details>


### [4] [Optimal Transport Flow Matching by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04092)
*Shimon Malnick,Matan Rusanovsky,Ohad Fried,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新的流匹配方法，通过选择低频率投影作为先验分布来实现最优运输的身份耦合，从而获得笔直且不交叉的轨迹，实现更高效的多步甚至单步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法在学习从简单先验分布到复杂数据分布的传输时，需要计算高维空间的最优运输耦合，这在计算上是难以处理的。现有方法要么需要求解最优运输问题（带来持续偏差或显著开销），要么使用的耦合不是最优运输耦合，导致学习的轨迹弯曲且交叉，限制了生成效率。

Method: 重新定义问题：将先验视为设计选择而非固定输入。提出使用自然图像的低频率投影作为先验分布，因为数据与其低频率表示之间的身份耦合在经验上是最优运输最优的。该先验既可以通过轻量模型在推理时采样，又保留了结构信息。剩余的任务简化为合成高频细节。此外，将先验与高斯噪声插值进一步改善生成质量同时保持最优运输耦合。

Result: 在所有基准测试中，该方法比现有流匹配方法减少了超过2倍的轨迹曲率，在少步生成（few-step）机制下获得更好的生成质量。该方法不需要修改流模型本身，并且自然集成到潜在空间模型、无分类器指导框架和单步生成框架中。

Conclusion: 通过将先验分布设计为数据样本的低频率投影，可以绕过难以计算的高维最优运输耦合问题，同时获得最优运输的身份耦合。这种方法不仅计算高效，还能产生笔直、不交叉的轨迹，显著改善了少步生成的质量和效率，为流匹配方法提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Flow matching models learn to transport samples from a simple prior distribution to a complex data distribution. When prior-data pairs are coupled via optimal transport (OT), the learned trajectories are straight and non-crossing, enabling fast, even single-step, generation. However, computing the OT coupling in high dimensions is intractable, and existing methods attempt to solve the OT problem, at the cost of persistent bias or significant overhead. Rather than solving for the OT coupling, we reformulate the problem. Once the prior is treated as a design choice rather than a fixed input, the OT coupling between prior and data is no longer unique. Many priors admit an OT-optimal identity coupling to the data, leaving us free to choose one that is also tractable to sample. We identify low-frequency projection of natural images as such a choice. The identity coupling between data and its low-frequency representation is empirically OT-optimal, the prior is structured enough to be sampled by a lightweight model at inference, and the remaining flow-matching task reduces to synthesizing high-frequency detail. Interpolating the prior with Gaussian noise further improves generation quality while preserving the OT coupling. The approach requires no modifications to the flow model itself, and integrates naturally with latent-space models, classifier-free guidance, and one-step generation frameworks. Across all benchmarks, our method reduces trajectory curvature by more than $2\times$ compared to existing flow matching methods, yielding better generation quality in the few-step regime.

</details>


### [5] [When Seeing Is Not Believing -- A Benchmark for Search-Grounded Video Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04098)
*Tao Yu,Yujia Yang,Shenghua Chai,Zhang Jinshuai,Haopeng Jin,Hao Wang,Minghui Zhang,Zhongtian Luo,Yuchen Long,Xinlong Chen,Jiabing Yang,Zhaolu Kang,Yuxuan Zhou,Zhengyu Man,Xinming Wang,Hongzhu Yi,Zheqi He,Xi Yang,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对语义和证据层面的视频虚假信息，研究者创建了EVID-Bench基准测试，要求系统必须通过检索开放网络中的相关视频并进行跨视频比较来识别虚假信息，现有前沿模型在此任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视频虚假信息已发展到语义和证据层面，仅凭输入视频本身无法可靠验证，因为缺失、重新排序、替换或重新语境化的证据都在视频之外。需要开发能够进行搜索增强的验证系统。

Method: 研究者构建了EVID-Bench基准测试，包含222个视频，涵盖AI生成、单源编辑和多源编辑3个类别共9种操纵类型。所有样本都经过验证，仅凭视觉检查无法被前沿模型检测到。评估了9个前沿多模态模型，并采用检索增强验证基准进行评估。

Result: 最佳系统仅达到61.43%的片段级准确率和43.24%的视频级准确率，AI生成的操纵尤其具有挑战性。错误分析揭示了模型常见的挑战：过度关注无关锚点、错误地将合成内容归因于编辑拼接、以及在完全解释操纵前过早终止搜索。

Conclusion: 基于证据的视频虚假信息检测极具挑战性，仅凭视觉检查无法有效检测，需要跨视频证据检索和比较能力。EVID-Bench基准为搜索增强的视频虚假信息检测提供了重要测试平台，揭示了当前模型的局限性和改进方向。

Abstract: Video misinformation increasingly operates at the semantic and evidential level: authentic footage may be selectively edited, temporally reordered, spliced across sources, or augmented with AI-generated content to construct false narratives. Such evidence-dependent manipulations cannot be reliably verified from the input video alone, because the missing, reordered, replaced, or recontextualized evidence lies outside the video itself. We introduce \textbf{EVID-Bench}, a benchmark for search-grounded video misinformation detection, where a system must search the open web for related videos and identify what information is false through cross-video comparison. EVID-Bench comprises 222 videos spanning 9 manipulation types across 3 categories: AI generation, single-source editing, and multi-source editing. All samples are verified to be undetectable by frontier models through visual inspection alone. We evaluate nine frontier multimodal models using a retrieval-augmented verification baseline. The best system achieves only 61.43\% point-level accuracy and 43.24\% video-level accuracy, while AI-generated manipulations remain especially challenging. Error analysis reveals recurring challenges: models fixate on irrelevant anchors, misattribute synthetic content to editorial splicing, and terminate search prematurely before fully explaining the manipulation.

</details>


### [6] [Reflection Separation from a Single Image via Joint Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04107)
*Zheng-Hui Huang,Zhixiang Wang,Yu-Lun Liu,Yung-Yu Chuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个针对单图像反射分离任务的扩散模型，通过微调利用生成扩散先验，在极端条件（眩光、弱反射）下实现鲁棒的分离效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单图像反射分离方法在极端条件（如眩光或弱反射）下难以恢复两个层，因为信息不足。这些场景下现有方法表现不佳，需要更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个专门为此任务微调的扩散模型，使用统一的扩散模型同时生成透射层和反射层。创新点包括：1）跨层自注意力机制以实现更好的特征解缠；2）分离采样策略在扩散过程中迭代减少层间干扰；3）潜在优化步骤结合学习的组合函数以改善复杂真实场景效果。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，该方法在多个真实世界基准测试中超越了最先进的方法，在极端条件下实现了更好的反射分离效果。

Conclusion: 提出的基于扩散模型的反射分离方法通过创新的跨层自注意力、分离采样策略和潜在优化，在极端条件下实现了鲁棒的单图像反射分离，为这一具有挑战性的任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Single-image reflection separation is highly challenging under extreme conditions like glare or weak reflections. Existing methods often struggle to recover both layers in glare or weak-reflection scenarios because of insufficient information. This paper presents a diffusion model explicitly fine-tuned for this task, leveraging generative diffusion priors for robust separation. Our method simultaneously generates transmission and reflection layers through a unified diffusion model, incorporating a novel cross-layer self-attention mechanism for better feature disentanglement. We further introduce a disjoint sampling strategy to iteratively reduce interference between the layers during diffusion and a latent optimization step with a learned composition function for improved results in complex real-world scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach surpasses state-of-the-art methods on multiple real-world benchmarks. Project page: https://brian90709.github.io/diff-reflection-separation/

</details>


### [7] [Pinpoint: Grounded Worldwide Image Geolocation via Cross-Source Retrieval and Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04133)
*Nika Chuzhoy,Brian Hu,Amit A. Arora,Jae Ro,Sarthak S. Sahu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pinpoint是一种结合普通互联网照片和街景图像的粗到精检索-重排序架构，用于图像地理定位任务，在两个不同数据源上实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像地理定位在全球尺度面临挑战，因为视觉证据模糊、多样且分布不均。现有工作将普通互联网照片和街景图像的定位视为独立任务，但两者具有互补优势：互联网照片更匹配用户拍摄查询的外观分布，而街景图像提供更密集、地理基础更强的覆盖。作者希望通过结合两者提升定位性能。

Method: 提出Pinpoint架构，包含两个阶段：1) 对比图像-GPS编码器，在Flickr照片和街景图像上训练，学习共享的图像-GPS嵌入空间用于候选位置检索；2) 基于注意力的重排序器，通过结合候选位置的视觉和GPS特征，以及来自附近位置的跨源证据，对检索到的候选进行重新评分。

Result: Pinpoint在标准基准测试中取得了最先进的结果：在互联网照片基准（IM2GPS3k和YFCC4k）和街景图像基准（OSV-5M）上的所有指标都达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: Pinpoint通过有效结合普通互联网照片和街景图像的互补优势，为图像地理定位任务提供了一种高效且可复现的解决方案，不依赖多模态大语言模型，实现更快的推理速度和更好的性能。

Abstract: Image geolocation aims to estimate where a photograph was taken from its visual content. At worldwide scale, this remains challenging because visual evidence is often ambiguous, diverse, and unevenly distributed. Prior work has typically treated geolocation of ordinary internet photos and street-view imagery as separate tasks, despite their complementary strengths: internet photos better match the appearance distribution of user-captured queries, while street-view imagery provides denser, geographically grounded coverage. We present Pinpoint, a retrieve-and-rerank architecture that combines both sources in a coarse-to-fine pipeline. A contrastive image-GPS embedder is trained on both user-uploaded Flickr photos and street-view imagery, learning a shared image-GPS embedding space that is used to retrieve candidate locations. An attention-based reranker then rescores retrieved candidates by combining candidate-level visual and GPS features with cross-source evidence from nearby locations to ground the prediction. Unlike recent prior work, Pinpoint does not rely on multimodal large-language models, making inference faster and more reproducible. Pinpoint achieves state-of-the-art results across all metrics on standard benchmarks for internet photos (IM2GPS3k and YFCC4k) and street-view imagery (OSV-5M).

</details>


### [8] [End-to-End Text Line Detection and Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04166)
*Benjamin Kiessling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Orli是一个端到端模型，将历史文档中的行检测和阅读顺序两个子任务统一为图像到序列问题，直接从页面图像自回归生成按阅读顺序排列的文本行基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统历史文档文本识别流程将布局分析分解为行检测和阅读顺序两个独立步骤，其中阅读顺序通常由手动编码的几何启发式方法处理，但这种方法难以处理页边注释、多栏、表格和特定源编辑惯例等复杂情况

Method: Orli采用端到端方法，将行检测和阅读顺序统一为单个图像到序列问题。模型使用弦帧参数化表示基线，通过锚定行的位置、方向和范围，同时通过垂直偏移编码局部几何。模型包含迭代细化头和局部视觉细化器生成最终曲线，采用自回归方式直接从页面图像生成按阅读顺序排列的文本行基线

Result: 在10种书写系统的196,691页异构语料库上训练，Orli在没有数据集特定训练的情况下略微超过了之前报告的cBAD行检测最先进结果；在多个阅读顺序基准测试中零样本实现了接近完美的覆盖率和排序；通过有限微调能够适应更专业的域外布局

Conclusion: Orli统一了传统流程中的行检测和阅读顺序步骤，展现了强大的泛化能力，为历史文档布局分析提供了一种更健壮、端到端的解决方案。模型代码和权重已开源发布

Abstract: Practical text-recognition pipelines for historical documents typically decompose layout analysis into line detection followed by a separate reading-order step, with the latter most often handled by a hand-coded geometric heuristic that struggles with marginalia, multiple columns, tables, and source-specific editorial conventions. This article introduces Orli (Ordered Regression of Lines), an end-to-end model that casts both sub-tasks as a single image-to-sequence problem: from a page image, Orli autoregressively generates text-line baselines directly in reading order. Baselines are represented in a chord-frame parameterization that anchors a line's position, orientation, and extent while encoding local geometry through perpendicular offsets; an iterative refinement head and a local visual refiner produce the final curve. Trained on a heterogeneous corpus of 196,691 pages spanning ten writing systems, Orli marginally exceeds the previously reported state of the art for cBAD line detection without dataset-specific training, reaches near perfect coverage and ordering on multiple reading-order benchmarks zero-shot, and adapts to more specialized out-of-domain layouts with limited fine-tuning. The method's source code and model weights are available under an open license at https://github.com/mittagessen/orli.

</details>


### [9] [GroupToM-Bench: Benchmarking Group Theory of Mind and Nonlinear Social Emergence in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04184)
*Weidong Tang,Jierui Li,Yueling Hou,Zihan Mei,Can Zhang,Xinyan Wan,Zhiyuan Liang,Pengfei Zhou,Yang You,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文介绍了GroupToM-Bench，这是第一个用于评估群体层面心理理论能力的多模态基准测试，揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在理解社会结构和非线性集体动态方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真正的通用智能不仅需要物理世界模型，还需要社会世界模型：能够推断个体心理状态如何相互作用并形成群体层面的结果。尽管个体层面的心理理论推理取得了显著进展，但现有的多模态大语言模型在这个更广泛的任务上失败了。集体行为从社会张力、从众动态和结构约束中非线性地涌现，这意味着它不能通过简单加总个体意图来恢复。

Method: 作者提出了GroupToM-Bench，这是第一个用于群体层面心理理论的多模态基准测试，建立在一个跨越三个层面的因果链上：微观层面的BDI状态（信念、欲望、意图）、中观层面的群体张力和结构约束，以及宏观层面的结果预测和机制归因。为了全面探测这个因果链，作者开发了一个七级认知审计框架。

Result: 实验揭示了当前模型与人类基线之间的差距，突显了模型在处理社会结构和非线性集体动态方面的失败。

Conclusion: 构建真正的社会世界模型是通用智能发展的关键挑战，需要超越个体层面的心理理论推理，深入理解群体层面的社会动态和结构约束。GroupToM-Bench为解决这一挑战提供了重要的评估工具和基准。

Abstract: True general intelligence requires not only a model of the physical world but also a social world model: the capacity to infer how individual mental states interact and crystallize into group-level outcomes. Despite notable progress in individual-level Theory of Mind (ToM) reasoning, existing multimodal large language models fail at this broader task. Collective behavior emerges non-linearly from social tensions, conformity dynamics, and structural constraints, meaning it cannot be recovered by merely summing individual intentions. We present GroupToM-Bench, the first multimodal benchmark for group-level ToM, built around a causal chain spanning micro-level BDI states (belief, desire, intention), meso-level group tension and structural constraints, and macro-level outcome prediction and mechanistic attribution. To probe this full arc, we develop a seven-level cognitive audit framework. Experiments reveal a gap between current models and human baselines, highlighting a failure to process social structures and non-linear collective dynamics.

</details>


### [10] [Spatial Artifact Coherence Determines Codec Robustness in Patch-Based rPPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04198)
*Achraf Ben Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的度量标准SAC（空间伪影相干性），用于预测在不同视频编解码器压缩下，基于空间分解的rPPG方法（如PCA）相对于全局投影方法的性能优势，并开发了PatchPCA算法家族，确定了部署最稳健的算法和适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程光电容积描记术（rPPG）在未压缩基准上已达到低心率误差，但在实际部署中（如远程医疗、新生儿ICU、驾驶员疲劳监测）通常使用压缩视频通道。此前没有工作能够确定决定空间分解方法在编解码器压缩下何时优于全局投影方法的物理量。

Method: 提出了空间伪影相干性（SAC）度量标准，定义为4×4跨块绿色通道协方差矩阵中非对角线与对角线能量之比（带通0.75-2.5Hz）。开发了PatchPCA算法家族（四种编解码器感知的rPPG算法）。评估了280名受试者、三个公共数据集、11种编解码器降级变体（MPEG-4、H.265、H.264、JPEG、色度子采样）和13种算法，使用Wilcoxon检验进行统计分析。

Result: SAC解释了PCA优势中93.8%的变体间方差（r=+0.969）。非MPEG-4编解码器变体在SAC 0.10-0.18范围内，PCA胜率为84-90%；MPEG-4变体在SAC 0.48-0.59范围内，胜率61%，平均改进减少5.8倍。78%的受试者内数据符合预期模式。P-Hybrid被确定为最稳健的部署算法。确定了PatchPCA优势的两个必要操作条件：SAC < 0.30和低到中等运动。

Conclusion: SAC提供了一个物理基础明确的度量标准，用于在临床远程监测系统中选择编解码器感知的rPPG算法。该研究揭示了MPEG-4的结构效应（宏块DCT几何结构），并直接排除了原始到MPEG-4转码流水线的适用性。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) achieves low heart-rate error on uncompressed benchmarks yet is deployed over compressed video channels in telehealth, neonatal ICU, and driver fatigue applications. No prior work identifies the physical quantity determining when spatial decomposition outperforms global-projection methods under codec compression.
  We propose Spatial Artifact Coherence (SAC), defined as the ratio of off-diagonal to diagonal energy in the 4x4 inter-patch Green-channel covariance matrix (bandpass 0.75-2.5 Hz), and the PatchPCA algorithm family (four codec-aware rPPG algorithms). We evaluate 280 subjects across three public datasets, 11 codec degradation variants (MPEG-4, H.265, H.264, JPEG, chroma subsampling), and 13 algorithms via Wilcoxon tests (BH-FDR, q < 0.05, 904 tests).
  SAC explains 93.8% of between-variant variance in PCA advantage (r = +0.969), with zero overlap between codec families: non-MPEG-4 variants cluster at SAC 0.10-0.18 with 84-90% PCA win rates, while MPEG-4 variants cluster at SAC 0.48-0.59 with 61% win rate and a 5.8x reduction in mean improvement. Within subjects, 78% confirm the expected pattern (p < 10^-22, dz = 0.73). Within-variant subject-level SAC correlation is r = +0.099, confirming SAC classifies codec families rather than predicting individual outcomes. MPEG-4's effect is structural (macroblock DCT geometry, not noise amplitude), governed by source codec state, not resolution.
  P-Hybrid is identified as the most deployment-robust algorithm. Two necessary operating conditions for PatchPCA advantage are established: SAC < 0.30 and low-to-moderate motion, directly ruling out raw-to-MPEG-4 transcoding pipelines. SAC provides a physically grounded metric for codec-aware rPPG algorithm selection in clinical remote monitoring systems.

</details>


### [11] [Overview of the EReL@MIR 2025 Multimodal Document Retrieval Challenge (Track 1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04240)
*Jingbiao Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 多模态文档检索挑战赛要求构建单一检索系统处理两个互补任务：基于文本查询的长文档内部页面检索（MMDocIR）和基于图像或图文查询的开放域维基百科式段落检索（M2KR），基于Qwen2-VL系列多模态大模型的解码器式嵌入器胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉丰富文档（包含文本与图表混合的页面）的检索对多模态检索增强生成至关重要，但当前大多数检索器仍丢弃视觉信息。该挑战旨在推动能够同时处理两种互补检索场景的单一检索系统的发展。

Method: 该报告描述了挑战赛的设计、数据集和评估协议。获胜团队均基于Qwen2-VL系列的解码器式多模态大模型嵌入器，而非CLIP风格的编码器，主要区别在于：1）微调集成方法；2）无训练的多路径融合配合强视觉语言重排序器；3）零延迟交互方法。

Result: 挑战赛吸引了455名参赛者和22个团队的586份提交。无训练系统仅以0.1分之差落后于微调获胜者，表明训练无关方法在该任务中具有强大竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于解码器的多模态大模型嵌入器在视觉丰富文档检索任务中表现出色，无训练方法也能达到接近微调系统的性能，为多模态检索系统设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Retrieval over visually-rich documents, pages that interleave text with figures, tables, and charts, is essential for multimodal retrieval-augmented generation, yet most retrievers still discard the visual channel. The \emph{Multimodal Document Retrieval Challenge}, Track~1 of the MIR Challenge at the first EReL@MIR workshop, co-located with The Web Conference 2025, asks participants to build a \emph{single} retrieval system that handles two complementary regimes: closed-set document page retrieval within long documents from a text query (MMDocIR), and open-domain retrieval of Wikipedia-style passages from an image or image-plus-text query (M2KR). Systems are ranked by the macro-average of mean Recall@$\{1,3,5\}$ over the two tasks. The challenge drew 455 entrants and 586 submissions across 22 teams. This report describes the challenge design, datasets, and evaluation protocol; reports the final standings; and analyses the three winning teams' systems. All three build on decoder-based Multimodal-LLM embedders from the Qwen2-VL family rather than on CLIP-style encoders, and differ chiefly in whether they reach the top through fine-tuned ensembles, training-free multi-route fusion with a strong vision-language re-ranker, or zero-shot late interaction. The training-free system finished within $0.1$ point of the fine-tuned winner.

</details>


### [12] [Prospective Dynamic 3D MRI Reconstruction via Latent-Space Motion Tracking from Single Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04249)
*Lixuan Chen,Zhongnan Liu,Jesse Hamilton,James M. Balter,Jeong Joon Park,Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PDMR是一种前瞻性动态3D MRI重建框架，通过潜在空间运动跟踪实现快速在线自适应，用于MRI引导的放疗等临床应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前瞻性重建在MRI引导的放疗等临床应用中至关重要，但由于超稀疏采样和严格的延迟要求，面临着巨大挑战。需要实现从当前获取的测量中准确重建图像和快速运动估计。

Method: 提出了PDMR（前瞻性动态3D MRI重建框架），核心思想是离线学习运动场的高效且可泛化的潜在流形，实现快速在线自适应。具体包括：1）在低维流形上参数化变形矢量场（DVFs），减少在线自适应的搜索空间；2）采用三平面表示实现几何感知和内存高效的3D运动编码。

Result: 在XCAT数字体模和内部腹部MRI数据集上的实验表明，PDMR在多个前瞻性场景（立即重建和2分钟后重建）中实现了高保真度和时间一致的重建，优于最先进的回顾性和在线方法。

Conclusion: PDMR为临床实践中实现超快速、运动感知的前瞻性MRI重建提供了有希望的途径。

Abstract: Prospective reconstruction is crucial in many clinical applications such as MRI-guided radiotherapy, which demands accurate image reconstruction and fast motion estimation from currently acquired measurements. However, prospective reconstruction remains challenging due to ultra-sparse sampling and stringent latency requirements. In this work, we propose PDMR, a Prospective Dynamic 3D MRI Reconstruction framework with latent-space motion tracking. Our core idea is to learn an efficient and generalizable latent manifold of motion fields offline, enabling rapid online adaptation for prospective reconstruction. Specifically, we parameterize the deformation vector fields (DVFs) on a low-dimensional manifold, effectively reducing the search space for fast online adaptation, and employ a tri-plane representation to achieve geometry-aware and memory-efficient encoding of 3D motion. Experiments on both XCAT digital phantoms and in-house abdominal MRI datasets demonstrate that PDMR achieves high-fidelity and temporally consistent reconstruction across multiple prospective scenarios (Immediate and After-2min), outperforming state-of-the-art retrospective and online methods. Our results suggest a promising pathway toward ultra-fast, motion-aware prospective MRI reconstruction in clinical practice.

</details>


### [13] [SBP-Net: Learning Thin Structure Reconstruction with Sliding-Box Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04251)
*Ofir Gilad,Andrei Sharf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于局部深度投影的薄壁3D结构重建方法，通过滑动窗口生成局部正交深度投影，用神经网络重建缺失的薄结构细节，再融合回3D模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄壁3D结构重建面临挑战：结构稀疏、尺度变化大、几何复杂。现有神经网络方法在密集表面上表现良好，但在恢复精细薄结构时往往失败。这类结构广泛存在于医疗血管成像和工业管道系统等领域。

Method: 采用基于局部深度投影的方法：1）使用滑动框遍历3D模型，生成局部正交深度投影作为2D表示；2）用神经网络处理这些投影，重建缺失的薄结构；3）将局部重建结果融合回3D模型，形成连贯的详细形状。

Result: 在肺动脉CT体积重建和工业管道（合成和真实扫描）恢复实验中，相比现有方法，该方法能更好地保留精细结构细节。

Conclusion: 提出的局部深度投影方法为薄壁3D结构重建提供了有效的解决方案，通过将3D问题转化为2D处理，克服了现有方法在恢复精细几何结构方面的局限性，在医疗和工业应用中都表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Reconstructing thin 3D structures is challenging due to their sparsity, scale variation, and complex geometry. Such structures arise in a wide range of domains, including medical imaging of vascular systems and industrial pipe systems. While recent neural methods perform well on dense surfaces, they often fail to recover fine thin geometries. We propose a reconstruction approach based on local depth projections, which provide an efficient and informative 2D representation of thin structures. Specifically, we traverse the 3D model with a sliding box to generate local orthographic depth projections, which are processed by a neural network to reconstruct missing thin structures in 2D. The local reconstructions are subsequently fused back into the 3D model to produce a coherent and detailed shape. Experiments on pulmonary artery reconstruction from CT volumes and industrial pipeline recovery from synthetic and real scans demonstrate improved preservation of fine structural details over existing methods.

</details>


### [14] [UniCanvas: A Diffusion-base Unified Model for Text-in-Image Joint Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04264)
*Zeyuan Yang,Hao-Wei Chen,Xueyang Yu,Yuncong Yang,Haoyu Zhen,Ziqiao Ma,Maohao Shen,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniCanvas提出了一种统一的扩散模型，通过在单一像素画布上"绘制"文本作为视觉模式来实现多模态内容生成，克服了传统方法在图像和文本生成之间的割裂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型存在生成能力的割裂：自回归VLMs能进行跨模态推理但无法生成高质量图像，而扩散模型能生成逼真图像但难以生成连贯文本。需要开发一个能无缝处理视觉和文本生成的统一模型。

Method: 提出UniCanvas模型，将语言作为图像内部的视觉模式进行表示，利用扩散模型固有的多模态嵌入空间。模型不是生成离散的文本标记，而是在图像合成过程中在单个像素画布上自然地"绘制"文本。

Result: 实验表明UniCanvas相比之前的统一模型有性能提升，证明了通过文本-图像生成的扩散模型作为统一多模态生成范式的潜力。

Conclusion: UniCanvas首次尝试统一扩散模型来生成交错的多模态内容，通过在图像中生成文本的方法，为统一的多模态生成提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent years have seen remarkable progress in unified vision-language models handling both multimodal understanding and generation within a single architecture. While autoregressive VLMs can reason across modalities, they fail to generate high-quality images. In contrast, diffusion models produce photorealistic visuals yet struggle to generate coherent text, making it challenging to develop a single unified model that can seamlessly handle both visual and text generation. Recent advances suggest that language can be effectively embedded within visual representations, allowing models to reason about textual semantics directly from images. To this end, we propose UniCanvas, a first attempt that unifies diffusion models to generate interleaved multimodal contents through text-in-image generation. Diffusion models naturally capture transformations on a shared pixel canvas, which can be viewed as world models of visual change. Instead of producing discrete text tokens, the model learns to represent language as visual patterns inside images, leveraging its inherent multimodal embedding space. This design allows the model to "draw" text naturally within a single pixel canvas during image synthesis, achieving seamless multimodal generation. Experiments demonstrate that UniCanvas improves performance over previous unified models, positioning text-in-image generation with diffusion models as a promising unified multimodal generation paradigm.

</details>


### [15] [StandardE2E: A Unified Framework for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04271)
*Stepan Konev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StandardE2E是一个端到端自动驾驶框架，通过统一数据接口支持多数据集预处理、跨数据集训练和简化数据集集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集存在文件格式、API、坐标系和模态覆盖不一致的问题，导致跨数据集实验需要为每个项目重新实现预处理，增加了开发和实验的复杂度。

Method: 提出StandardE2E框架：1) 为标准化的每数据集预处理提供统一数据模式；2) 在单个PyTorch DataLoader中合并多个数据集，支持跨数据集预训练、辅助任务监督和场景级过滤；3) 简化新数据集集成，只需将原始帧映射到规范模式即可。

Result: 框架已支持Waymo End-to-End、Waymo Perception、Argoverse 2 Sensor、Argoverse 2 LiDAR、NAVSIM (OpenScene-v1.1)和WayveScenes101六个数据集，并作为开源standard-e2e Python包发布。

Conclusion: StandardE2E通过统一接口解决了自动驾驶数据集的异构性问题，简化了端到端模型开发流程，促进了跨数据集实验和模型评估。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has shifted from modular perception-prediction-planning stacks toward end-to-end (E2E) models that map sensor inputs directly to vehicle control, often regularized by auxiliary tasks such as 3D detection, motion forecasting, and HD-map perception. Progress is driven by a fast-growing ecosystem of sensor-rich driving datasets, yet each ships its own file formats, APIs, coordinate conventions, and modality coverage, leaving cross-dataset experimentation and even basic per-dataset preprocessing to be re-implemented per project. We present StandardE2E, a framework that provides a single unified interface over E2E driving datasets. StandardE2E (i) standardizes per-dataset preprocessing under one shared data schema; (ii) combines multiple datasets in a single PyTorch DataLoader for cross-dataset pretraining, auxiliary-task supervision, and scenario-level filtering; and (iii) reduces adding a new dataset to a single per-dataset mapping from raw frames to the canonical schema, leaving the entire downstream pipeline unchanged. The framework supports six datasets out of the box: Waymo End-to-End, Waymo Perception, Argoverse 2 Sensor, Argoverse 2 LiDAR, NAVSIM (OpenScene-v1.1), and WayveScenes101, and is released as the open-source standard-e2e Python package, available at https://github.com/stepankonev/StandardE2E.

</details>


### [16] [FindIt: A Format-Informed Visual Detection Benchmark for Generalist Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04282)
*Eshika Khandelwal,Jingjing Pan,Mingfang Zhang,Quan Kong,Lorenzo Garattoni,Hilde Kuehne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了第一个专门评估通用多模态大语言模型可提示定位能力的综合基准，涵盖物体检测、引用表达式检测、实例级检测和视频检测四个核心任务类别，并建立了统一的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的实际应用正快速扩展到结构化计算机视觉设置中，如需要定位能力的物体检测任务。然而，目前缺乏标准化基准来系统评估这些能力，这限制了MLLMs在定位中心任务中的发展和评估。

Method: 开发了一个统一的评估框架：1）标准化输入格式；2）强制执行可解析的边界框输出；3）定义跨任务的透明评估协议。基于此框架建立了包含四个核心任务类别的基准：物体检测、引用表达式检测、实例级检测和视频检测。

Result: 评估了多样化的开源和专有MLLMs，发现当前系统对输出格式约束高度敏感，即使面对微小的格式变化也常常无法泛化。结果揭示了先进MLLMs在定位设置中的优势和不足，特别是在格式一致性和泛化能力方面存在显著缺陷。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了首个专门评估MLLMs定位能力的综合基准，揭示了当前模型在格式敏感性和泛化能力方面的局限性，为改进多模态模型设计和评估指出了重要方向，特别是在结构化计算机视觉任务中的应用。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are predominantly evaluated on free-form vision-language tasks such as visual question answering, captioning, and summarization. However, their practical use is rapidly expanding to more structured computer vision settings, where users prompt models to perform localization-centric tasks such as object detection, often within larger agentic or decision-making systems. Despite this shift, there is currently no standardized benchmark that systematically evaluates these capabilities at scale. In this work, we introduce the first comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to assess the promptable localization abilities of generalist MLLMs. Our benchmark spans four core task categories: object detection, referring expression detection, instance-level detection, and video-based detection. To enable consistent and fair evaluation, we develop a unified framework that standardizes inputs, enforces parsable bounding box outputs, and defines transparent evaluation protocols across tasks. Using this suite, we evaluate a diverse set of open-source and proprietary MLLMs, providing an in-depth analysis of their performance and limitations. Beyond accuracy, we examine models' ability to adhere to output format specifications, showing that current systems are highly sensitive to formatting constraints and often fail to generalize even to minor variations. Our results highlight both the strengths and shortcomings of state-of-the-art MLLMs in localization settings, and point toward important directions for improving multimodal model design and evaluation.

</details>


### [17] [A Cookbook of 3D Vision: Data, Learning Paradigms, and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04291)
*Hongyang Du,Zongxia Li,Dawei Liu,Runhao Li,Haoyuan Song,Qingyu Zhang,Yubo Wang,Jingcheng Ni,Shihang Gui,Congchao Dong,Tao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一个以数据为中心的三维视觉分类法，连接了几何表示、数据集、学习框架和应用，提供了统一视角来分析三维视觉领域的效率、保真度和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维视觉领域快速发展，但存在表示方法和基准测试的碎片化问题，难以形成关于效率、保真度和可扩展性的统一视角。

Method: 提出了一个数据中心的分类法，分析点云、网格、体素和三维高斯等主要几何表示及其采集流程，研究数据集设计、基准构建和监督机制如何塑造最新进展。

Result: 通过集成视角，阐明了表示方法、学习范式和下游任务（重建、生成、视频建模）之间的关系，提供了对平衡效率与保真度、多模态几何基础等新兴趋势的统一视图。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维视觉领域提供了一个统一的概念框架，连接了几何表示、数据集、学习框架和应用，帮助研究者更好地理解和评估该领域的发展方向。

Abstract: 3D vision has rapidly evolved, driven by increasingly diverse data representations, learning paradigms, and modeling strategies. Yet the field remains fragmented across representations and benchmarks, making it difficult to develop unified perspectives on efficiency, fidelity, and scalability. This work provides a data-centric taxonomy of 3D vision that connects geometric representations, datasets, learning frameworks, and applications within a single conceptual map. We begin by analysing the principal structural representations of 3D data--point clouds, meshes, voxels, and 3D Gaussians--along with their acquisition pipelines. We then examine how dataset design, benchmark construction, and supervision regimes shape recent advances, spanning 2D-supervised 3D learning, implicit neural representations, and 4D world modeling. Through this integrative lens, we clarify the relationships among representations, learning paradigms, and downstream tasks in reconstruction, generation, and video modeling, offering a consolidated view of emerging trends toward balancing efficiency and fidelity and toward multimodal geometric grounding.

</details>


### [18] [Efficient and Training-Free Single-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04299)
*Haojun Qiu,Kiriakos N. Kutulakos,David B. Lindell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的基于补丁的单图像扩散生成方法，通过封闭形式的去噪器实现快速高质量图像生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单图像训练的扩散模型需要数小时优化且计算成本高，需要开发更高效的训练自由方法

Method: 使用多尺度补丁数据集建模图像，利用最优封闭形式去噪器计算含噪补丁的得分函数，无需神经网络训练

Result: 相比训练的单图像扩散模型达到SOTA生成质量和多样性，支持无条件生成、文本引导风格化、图像对称化等应用，实现秒级兆像素和分钟级千兆像素生成

Conclusion: 基于补丁的训练自由扩散模型在单图像生成任务中优于现有训练方法，提供了高效且高质量的生成解决方案

Abstract: We consider the problem of generating images whose internal structure -- defined by the distribution of patches across multiple scales -- matches that of a single reference image. Recent approaches address this problem by training a diffusion model on a single image. But even in this setting, training is computationally expensive and requires hours of optimization. Instead, we model the image using a dataset of its patches at different scales. As this dataset is finite and the dimensionality of its patches is small, the score function for a noisy patch can be computed tractably using an optimal, closed-form denoiser, eliminating the need for neural network training. We integrate this patch-based denoiser into an efficient, training-free image diffusion model, and we describe how our method connects to classical patch-based image restoration techniques. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and diversity compared to trained single-image diffusion models, and we demonstrate applications, including unconditional image generation, text-guided stylization, image symmetrization, and retargeting. Further, we show that our approach is compatible with latent space diffusion, and we show multiple additional acceleration techniques to achieve megapixel single-image generation in one second, and gigapixel generation in minutes.

</details>


### [19] [XSSR: Cross-Domain Self-Supervised Representative Selection for Efficient Annotation in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04301)
*Byunghyun Ko,Aleksei Anisimov,Kobe Ke,Suhas Bharthepude,Jeongkyu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XSSR是一个跨域自监督代表选择框架，通过在源域预训练MAE、设计新颖性-多样性评分算法选择目标域样本，以及自动校准超参数，实现在仅5%标注预算下达到接近全数据性能的医学图像分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像标注成本高昂，尤其在跨域场景中，源域和目标域在成像设备、人群或临床地点上存在差异，这增加了获取标注数据的难度。现有方法需要大量目标域标注或手动调参，限制了实际应用。

Method: 1) 在无标注的源域数据上训练Masked Autoencoder（MAE）建立共享嵌入空间；2) 基于复合密度、新颖性和多样性准则，采用贪婪选择算法为无标注目标域样本评分并选择代表性子集；3) 仅在选定子集上训练U-Net分割模型。新颖性-多样性权衡参数α通过最小化嵌入空间覆盖率自动校准。

Result: 在三个公开基准测试中：Chest X-ray仅需22个标注样本即达到99.3%的全数据性能；Prostate MRI上比随机选择提升最多2.5个Dice点；在所有数据集上均优于CoreSet基线（0.4-1.2 Dice点提升）。消融研究表明多样性是最重要的评分成分，跨站点分析显示性能与扫描仪相似度正相关。

Conclusion: XSSR框架通过自监督学习和智能样本选择，显著减少了跨域医学图像分割的标注需求，实现了在有限标注预算下的高效模型训练，为实际临床部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Acquiring labeled medical image data is resource-intensive and a challenge further exacerbated in cross-domain scenarios where source and target datasets differ in imaging equipment, population, or clinical site. This study introduces XSSR (Cross-Domain Self-Supervised Representative Selection), a framework designed to minimize annotation effort in the target domain while maintaining robust segmentation performance. XSSR comprises three stages: first, a Masked Autoencoder (MAE) is trained on unlabeled source data to establish a shared embedding space without requiring target labels; second, a greedy selection algorithm scores unlabeled target samples based on a composite density, novelty, and diversity criterion; and third, a U-Net segmentation model is trained exclusively on the selected subset. The novelty-diversity trade-off parameter, alpha, is automatically calibrated by minimizing embedding-space coverage, eliminating manual tuning. We evaluate XSSR on three public benchmarks: Chest X-ray, RIGA+ retinal fundus imaging, and multi-site Prostate MRI, each under a fixed 5% annotation budget. XSSR achieves 99.3% of full-data performance on Chest X-ray using only 22 labeled samples, surpasses random selection by up to 2.5 Dice points on Prostate MRI, and consistently outperforms the CoreSet baseline by 0.4 to 1.2 Dice points across all datasets. Ablation studies indicate that diversity is the most influential scoring component, and per-site analysis shows that performance correlates with scanner similarity to the source domain.

</details>


### [20] [Answer Self-Consistency with Margin-Triggered Question Re-Arbitration for the CVPR 2026 VidLLMs Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04323)
*Tomoya Miyazawa,Hiroyasu Okuno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ASC-MQRA训练免费推理框架，通过答案自一致性提升视频关系推理性能，最终采用ASC组件（无再仲裁）在CVPR 2026挑战赛中取得优异成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVPR 2026 VidLLMs挑战赛Track 2需要模型推理视频中不总是显式可见的关系，现有方法在不确定性示例上存在性能瓶颈，需要更稳健的推理机制。

Method: 提出ASC-MQRA框架：ASC组件进行多次随机视频问答运行并通过答案级自一致性聚合答案；MQRA组件针对低置信度示例进行条件再仲裁，缩小候选集并重新观看视频。

Result: ASC显著优于单次推理；MQRA在验证集上进一步提升但测试集略有下降；最终ASC在验证集平均准确率72.73%，类别平均准确率78.34%；测试集平均准确率81.16%，类别平均准确率80.91%。

Conclusion: ASC框架有效提升视频关系推理性能，低置信度投票分布可作为有用不确定性信号，但再仲裁对触发子集大小和类别分布敏感；最终提交采用无再仲裁的ASC组件。

Abstract: In this report, we present our solution for Track 2 of the CVPR 2026 VidLLMs Challenge. This track evaluates visual relational reasoning in videos, where models must infer relations that are not always explicitly visible. We propose Answer Self-Consistency with Margin-Triggered Question Re-Arbitration (ASC-MQRA), a training-free test-time reasoning framework built on a multimodal reasoning model. The core ASC component performs multiple stochastic video question-answering runs and aggregates their answer choices through answer-level self-consistency. This substantially improves over single-pass inference and forms our final test submission. We further study MQRA, a conditional re-arbitration module for low-margin examples where the first-stage vote distribution indicates uncertainty. Our vote-margin analysis shows that low-margin examples often retain the ground-truth answer among the top candidates, motivating MQRA to narrow the candidate set and re-watch the video only over the retained candidates. On validation, MQRA further improves over ASC, indicating that low-margin vote distributions can provide a useful uncertainty signal. On test, however, MQRA slightly degrades performance relative to ASC, suggesting that re-arbitration is sensitive to the size and category distribution of the triggered subset. Our final test submission therefore uses ASC without re-arbitration, achieving 72.73 average accuracy and 78.34 category-wise macro average accuracy on validation, and 81.16 average accuracy and 80.91 category-wise macro average accuracy on test. This report details our prompting strategy, implementation setup, ablation studies, and diagnostic analyses. The code is available at https://github.com/data-analytics-labo/ASC-MQRA

</details>


### [21] [Robust Multi-view Clustering against Imperfect Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04343)
*Zhichao Huang,Haochen Zhou,Hao Wang,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为PLCI的新型多视图聚类框架，能够统一处理不完整视图和噪声对应关系这两种常见的不完美信息问题，通过后验引导的潜在对应推断来有效解决多视图数据中的跨视图信息缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的多视图数据通常存在不完美信息问题：一方面视图特定观测可能缺失（不完整视图，IV），另一方面跨视图对应关系可能存在错配（噪声对应，NC）。现有的IV和NC导向的多视图聚类方法要么需要可靠的对应关系，要么需要足够完整的实例，无法统一解决这两种不完美信息问题。作者观察到IV和NC都源于相同的跨视图对应信息不完美问题——锚实例在其他视图中的对应实例要么不可用，要么不可靠。

Method: 提出后验引导的潜在对应推断（PLCI）框架，将每个锚实例的期望跨视图对应视为潜在变量，通过整合实例级可靠性和原型级语义传输来推断潜在对应的后验分布。这种方法能够统一处理IV和NC问题。

Result: 在6个广泛使用的多视图数据集上进行了大量实验，与10种最先进的多视图聚类方法进行比较，结果表明PLCI在解决不完美信息问题方面具有有效性。

Conclusion: PLCI框架能够统一处理多视图数据中的不完整视图和噪声对应关系问题，通过推断潜在对应的后验分布有效解决了跨视图对应信息不完美的挑战，为实际应用中存在信息缺失的多视图聚类提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world multi-view data always suffer from imperfect information problem, where the view-specific observations are absent (i.e., Incomplete Views, IV) and cross-view correspondences are mismatched (i.e., Noisy Correspondences, NC) for certain instances. As a remedy, numerous IV- and NC-oriented multi-view clustering (MvC) methods have been proposed, which however require either reliable correspondences or sufficiently complete instances, thus stopping short of addressing the imperfect information problem. In contrast, we observe that both IV and NC challenges originate from the same issue of imperfect cross-view counterpart information, where the counterpart of an anchor instance in another view might be either unavailable or unreliable. Based on the observation, we propose a novel robust MvC framework, termed Posterior-guided Latent Counterpart Inference (PLCI), which could handle both IV and NC in a unified manner. Specifically, PLCI formulates the desired cross-view counterpart of each anchor instance as a latent variable, and integrates both instance-level reliability and prototype-level semantic transport to infer the posterior distribution of the latent counterpart. Extensive experiments on six widely-used multi-view datasets against 10 state-of-the-art MvC methods demonstrate the effectiveness of PLCI for tackling the imperfect information problem. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [22] [HYolo: An Intelligent IoT-Based Object Detection System Using Hypergraph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04345)
*Isha Abid,Fawad Khan,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HYolo：将超图学习集成到YOLO架构中的智能物联网物体检测框架，通过建模高阶特征关系提升上下文理解和检测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的YOLO物体检测模型主要捕获成对特征交互，但无法建模物体和上下文特征之间复杂的高阶关系，限制了上下文依赖性的捕获和物体表示的质量

Method: 提出HYolo框架，将超图学习集成到YOLO架构中，通过超图结构捕获丰富的上下文依赖关系，改进物体表示，建模高阶特征关系

Result: 在COCO数据集上的实验评估显示性能显著提升：相比基线YOLO模型，mAP@50提升约12%，整体检测精度和鲁棒性得到增强

Conclusion: 超图学习集成到物体检测管道为智能和上下文感知的物联网视觉系统提供了有前景的方向，HYolo通过建模高阶特征关系提高了上下文理解能力和检测可靠性

Abstract: This paper presents HYolo, an intelligent IoT-based object detection framework that integrates hypergraph learning into the YOLO architecture. Traditional YOLO-based object detection models primarily capture pairwise feature interactions and may fail to model complex high-order relationships among objects and contextual features. To address this limitation, HYolo incorporates hypergraph learning to capture richer contextual dependencies and improve object representation. Experimental evaluation on the COCO dataset demonstrates significant performance improvements over baseline YOLO models. The proposed approach achieves approximately 12% improvement in mAP@50 while enhancing overall detection accuracy and robustness. By modeling high-order feature relationships, HYolo provides improved contextual understanding and more reliable object detection performance in IoT-based environments. The results indicate that integrating hypergraph learning into object detection pipelines offers a promising direction for intelligent and context-aware IoT vision systems.

</details>


### [23] [MorphoQuant: Modality-Aware Quantization for Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04349)
*Yue Wu,Changyuan Wang,Zixuan Wang,Shilin Ma,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MorphoQuant是一个模态感知的后训练量化框架，针对4位全模态大语言模型中的分布异质性和异常值问题，通过选择性偏差补偿和形态导向的量化函数优化，显著提升了量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的后训练量化方法在处理4位全模态大语言模型时面临两个主要挑战：1）极端分布异质性（不同模态的数据分布差异极大）；2）跨模态的异常值模式不一致。这些问题导致标准量化方法在全模态场景下效果不佳。

Method: 提出了MorphoQuant框架，包含两个核心技术：1）分布感知偏差补偿(DABC)：选择性地将长尾异常值吸收到通道级偏差中，保护异常值幅度同时保持密集内点的高精度离散化；2）形态导向量化函数优化(MDQFO)：将量化网格与偏差掩码协同优化，确保跨模态的细粒度对齐。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni模型上的广泛评估显示，W4A4配置在ScienceQA上达到76.63%，显著优于现有的W4A4方法，并且超越了W4A16基线，展示了卓越的精度-效率权衡。在MMMU和Video-MME等基准测试中也表现出优越性。

Conclusion: MorphoQuant通过模态感知的量化策略有效解决了全模态大语言模型的量化挑战，实现了高精度4位量化，为实际部署提供了高效的解决方案，展现了在精度和效率之间的出色平衡能力。

Abstract: Conventional Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) methods struggle with 4-bit Omni-modal Large Language Models (OLLMs) due to the extreme distribution heterogeneity and disparate outlier patterns across modalities. To address this, we propose MorphoQuant, a modality-aware PTQ framework engineered to preserve cross-modal morphology and mitigate outlier loss. Specifically, we introduce Distribution-Aware Bias Compensation (DABC), which selectively absorbs long-tailed outliers into channel-wise biases. This mechanism safeguards outlier magnitudes while maintaining high-precision discretization for dense inliers, thereby preserving accurate discretization across diverse modal distribution. Complementing this, we propose Morphology-Directed Quantization Function Optimization (MDQFO) to co-optimize the quantization grid with the bias mask, ensuring fine-grained alignment across modalities. Extensive evaluations on Qwen2.5-Omni across benchmarks like MMMU and Video-MME demonstrate our approach's superiority. Notably, our W4A4 model achieves 76.63% on ScienceQA, significantly outperforming SOTA W4A4 methods and surprisingly surpassing the W4A16 baseline, which fully demonstrates the exceptional accuracy-efficiency trade-off of our framework.

</details>


### [24] [Video2LoRA: Parametric Video Internalization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04351)
*Manan Suri,Sarvesh Baskar,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video2LoRA：一种通过单次前向传播从视频直接生成LoRA适配器的参数化视频内部化方法，显著降低视频语言模型的推理成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型处理视频成本高昂，每帧占据数百个token，推理成本随帧数和查询次数线性增长

Method: 使用感知器超网络读取冻结VLM逐层编码视频时产生的中间表示，在单次前向传播中生成LoRA适配器权重，而非传统的迭代梯度更新

Result: 在SmolVLM2 500M和2.2B模型上的视频摘要和字幕任务中，Video2LoRA实现零视觉token推理，在5个字幕基准和7/8个VQA基准上表现非劣于直接视频上下文推理；减少视觉token负载1500倍，查询TTFT降低6-80倍，支持最多1024帧和1024px

Conclusion: Video2LoRA为高效视频处理提供了一种参数化内部化方案，非重叠视频段的独立适配器可在秩空间组合，为分块长视频内部化指明了方向

Abstract: Processing video in vision-language models is expensive: each frame occupies hundreds of tokens, and inference cost scales with every frame and every repeated query. We introduce Video2LoRA, a method for parametric video internalization. A perceiver hypernetwork reads the intermediate representations produced layer-by-layer as a frozen VLM encodes a video, and generates a Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapter in a single forward pass. Unlike standard LoRA fine-tuning, which requires iterative gradient updates, Video2LoRA predicts these weights directly from the video. Trained for SmolVLM2 500M and 2.2B on video summarization and captioning, Video2LoRA enables the same frozen VLM to answer queries from the adapter alone, with zero visual tokens in its context at query time. Video2LoRA is statistically non-inferior and equivalent to direct video-in-context inference across all five captioning benchmarks at both model scales, and across seven of eight video question answering benchmark-scale pairings. Although trained only on 12 frames at 384px, it remains stable up to 1,024 frames and 1024px, where direct video-in-context inference often degenerates. Across this sweep, it reduces answer-time visual-token load by up to 1,500x and query TTFT by 6-80x, while preserving video-faithful outputs. We also find that independently generated adapters for non-overlapping video segments can compose in rank space, suggesting a path toward chunked long-video internalization.

</details>


### [25] [Spatially Grounded Concept Bottleneck Models via Part-Factorized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04364)
*Dhanesh Ramachandram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部分的因子化概念瓶颈模型（CBM），通过空间先验和部分注意力机制，在无需每张图像关键点监督的情况下，提高细粒度识别的可解释性和指向准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）在细粒度识别任务中，概念头可以关注图像的任何区域，导致以身体部位命名的概念头可能被其他部位的证据满足，这降低了模型决策的可审计性和可解释性。本文旨在通过构建部分因子化的CBM来消除这种自由度。

Method: 方法基于冻结的DINOv3视觉Transformer构建，包含三个核心组件：1）学习的前景门控，基于DINOv3补丁特征抑制背景补丁；2）一组部分查询通过交叉注意力机制与补丁特征交互，312个CUB属性通过固定的概念-部分映射表只从与其名称对应的部分令牌读取信息；3）可学习的二维高斯先验，以加法方式注入注意力对数空间，打破部分查询之间的置换对称性，其均值从数据集平均关键点位置初始化，无需训练或测试时的每张图像关键点监督。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011数据集上，空间先验模型与全监督基线性能相当（88.85% vs 88.95% top-1准确率），同时将指向准确率提高了16个百分点（52.6% vs 36.4%）。用PCA前景目标替换边界框监督并结合高斯先验，可以完全消除每张图像监督，达到88.6% top-1准确率和约70%的指向准确率。关键点分数扫描显示，仅需训练集的0.5%（约27张图像）来初始化先验即可，无明显性能损失。完全移除部分身份时，指向准确率崩溃至2.9%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的部分因子化CBM通过在注意力机制中引入空间先验，成功提高了细粒度识别的可解释性和指向准确性，同时显著减少了监督需求。实验表明，即使仅使用极少的关键点标注，也能有效初始化空间先验并保持模型性能，为构建更透明和可审计的视觉识别系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) predict a layer of human-named attributes before predicting a class, which makes their decisions auditable. On fine-grained recognition tasks the concept heads are usually free to attend anywhere in the image, so a head named for one body region can be satisfied by evidence on another. This work studies a part-factorized CBM that removes that freedom by construction. The method has three components built on a frozen DINOv3 vision transformer. A learned foreground gate, trained on DINOv3 patch features, suppresses background patches inside the part attention. A set of part queries cross-attends to patch features and each of the 312 CUB attributes is routed, through a fixed concept-to-part map, to read only from the part token its name implies. A learnable two-dimensional Gaussian prior, injected additively in log space into the attention logits, breaks the permutation symmetry among part queries; its means are initialized from the dataset-average keypoint location of each part, which requires no per-image keypoint supervision at training or test time. On CUB-200-2011 the spatial-prior model matches a fully supervised baseline (88.85% versus 88.95% top-1) while raising pointing accuracy by 16 points (52.6% versus 36.4%). Replacing bounding-box supervision with a PCA foreground target and combining it with the Gaussian prior removes all per-image supervision and reaches 88.6% top-1 at about 70% pointing accuracy. A keypoint-fraction sweep shows that 0.5% of the training set (about 27 images) suffices to initialize the prior with no measurable loss. Removing part identity entirely is the harder case: without any spatial prior, pointing accuracy collapses to $2.9\%$.

</details>


### [26] [Multi-Granularity 3D Kidney Lesion Characterization from CT Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04365)
*Renjie Liang,Zhengkang Fan,Jinqian Pan,Chenkun Sun,Jiang Bian,Russell Terry,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LesionDETR：一种用于肾CT病灶表征的DETR风格架构，通过匈牙利匹配和分层损失实现每个病灶的集合预测，在内部和外部数据集上达到良好的侧级异常检测性能，但实体病灶检测仍是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D方法仅能在患者或器官级别进行预测，而放射学报告通常描述肾病灶的类型、大小、增强和衰减等临床属性。需要一种能够输出每个病灶多个临床属性的方法，以实现结构化报告生成。

Method: 将肾CT表征重新定义为每个病灶的集合预测任务：一个模型为每个肾输出可变数量的病灶，每个病灶带有四个临床属性。提出LesionDETR，这是一种DETR风格架构，采用大小-距离匈牙利匹配和分层损失（聚合每个槽位输出到侧级目标）。研究了四种输入表示和六种编码器初始化策略。

Result: 在内部数据集（UF-Health）上双侧侧级异常检测AUC为0.799±0.009，在外部数据集（KiTS23）上为0.817±0.072。对于囊性病灶，每个病灶的mAP为0.190±0.083。罕见的实体病灶AP保持在噪声水平，表明架构不是主要瓶颈，而是需要针对性的数据收集。

Conclusion: LesionDETR框架能够生成经过验证的每个病灶预测，用于下游结构化报告生成。关键设计选择包括使用分割掩码作为输入通道和相同领域的腹部预训练。罕见实体病灶的检测性能表明，下一步瓶颈在于有针对性的数据收集，而非架构改进。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe kidney lesions by type, size, enhancement, and attenuation, yet existing 3D methods predict only at the patient or organ level. We reformulate kidney CT characterization as a per-lesion set-prediction task: one model emits a variable number of lesions per kidney, each with four clinical attributes. We curated 2,619 CT volumes from 788 patients at one academic medical center, with multi-granularity side- and per-lesion labels, and used KiTS23 (489 cases) for zero-shot external validation. We propose \textbf{LesionDETR}, a DETR-style architecture with size-distance Hungarian matching and a hierarchical loss that aggregates per-slot outputs to side-level objectives. Across four input representations and six encoder initializations, two design choices dominate: a segmentation mask as an input channel, and same-domain abdominal pretraining (SuPreM); generic large-corpus pretraining is no better than random initialization. LesionDETR reaches bilateral side-level abnormality AUC $0.799 \pm 0.009$ on UF-Health and $0.817 \pm 0.072$ on KiTS23. A count-conditioned variant reaches per-lesion mAP $0.190 \pm 0.083$ on cystic lesions; rare solid-lesion AP stays at the noise floor, pointing to targeted data collection, not architecture, as the next bottleneck. The framework yields verified per-lesion predictions for downstream structured report generation.

</details>


### [27] [VT-3DAD: Cross-Category 3D Anomaly Detection via Visual-Text Normal Space Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04369)
*Zi Wang,Katsuya Hotta,Yawen Zou,Koichiro Kamide,Yijin Wei,Chao Zhang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VT-3DAD：一种基于视觉-文本正常空间对齐的训练无关框架，用于跨类别3D异常检测，通过融合多视图视觉特征和文本语义约束实现少样本异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的少样本跨类别3D异常检测方法存在两个主要问题：1）基于训练的方法需要类别级别的优化，效率低；2）基于多视图CLIP视觉特征的训练无关方法主要依赖视觉相似性，容易被几何相似的类别混淆。

Method: 提出了VT-3DAD框架：1）从点云生成真实多视图深度图，使用冻结CLIP视觉编码器提取视图特征；2）视觉分支测量参考-测试在多视图特征空间的偏差；3）同时使用深度感知和3D感知提示通过CLIP文本编码器构建文本正常锚点，为目标类别提供语义正常约束；4）融合视觉偏差和语义偏差得到最终异常分数。

Result: 在ShapeNetPart数据集上的实验表明，VT-3DAD取得了最先进的性能。具体来说，与仅视觉基线相比，将单样本平均AUC-ROC从92.49%提升到94.80%，同时将平均标准差从5.64降低到3.41。

Conclusion: VT-3DAD通过将视觉特征和文本语义信息相结合，有效解决了现有方法在少样本跨类别3D异常检测中的局限性，实现了更高的准确性和稳定性，同时避免了训练过程的需求。

Abstract: Few-shot cross-category 3D anomaly detection aims to determine whether an unknown point cloud belongs to a target normal category using only a few normal references. Existing training-based methods usually require category-wise optimization, while recent training-free methods based on multi-view CLIP visual features mainly rely on visual similarity and may be confused by geometrically similar categories. In this paper, we propose VT-3DAD, a training-free framework for cross-category 3D anomaly detection via Visual-Text Normal Space Alignment. Given few-shot normal references and a test point cloud, VT-3DAD first generates realistic multi-view depth maps and extracts view-wise features using a frozen CLIP visual encoder. The visual branch measures reference-test deviation in the multi-view feature space. In parallel, depth-aware and 3D-aware prompts are encoded by the frozen CLIP text encoder to construct textual normal anchors, which provide semantic normality constraints for the target category. The final anomaly score is obtained by fusing visual deviation from normal references and semantic deviation from the textual normal space. Experiments on the ShapeNetPart dataset demonstrate that VT-3DAD achieves state-of-the-art performance. In particular, VT-3DAD improves the one-shot average AUC-ROC from 92.49% to 94.80% compared with the visual-only baseline, while also reducing the average standard deviation from 5.64 to 3.41.

</details>


### [28] [Selective Coupling of Decoupled Informative Regions: Masked Attention Alignment for Data-Free Quantization of Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04373)
*Biao Qian,Yang Wang,Yong Wu,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MaskAQ方法，一种用于Vision Transformers的无数据量化新方法，通过掩蔽注意力对齐来解决合成样本与量化模型输入分布不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Vision Transformers无数据量化方法存在合成样本与量化模型期望输入分布不匹配的问题，导致性能下降。这主要是因为自注意力机制中语义信息主要集中在稀疏的补丁子集（信息区域）上，而这些信息区域主导了合成样本与量化模型输出之间的互信息。

Method: 提出MaskAQ方法：1) 通过最大化补丁相似度的差分熵，从噪声背景中解耦信息区域；2) 使用掩蔽注意力对齐目标，使信息区域在不同精度模型间对齐；3) 引入周期性样本刷新策略，使方法能适应量化模型在训练过程中的演化状态。

Result: 在多个主干网络和下游任务上的广泛实验验证了MaskAQ相对于最先进方法的优势。

Conclusion: MaskAQ通过揭示并利用Vision Transformers中信息区域的稀疏特性，以及设计有效的对齐和适应策略，成功解决了无数据量化中的分布不匹配问题，实现了高质量的合成样本生成和优异的量化性能。

Abstract: Data-Free Quantization (DFQ) addresses data security concerns by synthesizing samples, without accessing real data. It has garnered increasing attention in the context of Vision Transformers (ViTs), owing to the superiority of the self-attention mechanism compared to classical convolutional operation. However, previous DFQ arts for ViTs often suffer from a distribution mismatch between synthetic samples and input distribution expected by quantized models Q, resulting in the suboptimal performance. In this paper, we propose a novel Masked Attention Alignment approach for Data-Free Quantization of ViTs, named MaskAQ, revealing that: 1) the semantics in the self-attention mechanism is predominantly localized to a sparse subset of patches, called informative regions; 2) the informative regions dominate the mutual information between synthetic samples and Q's outputs. To these ends, we incorporate differential entropy maximum over patch similarity of synthetic samples, to decouple informative regions from noisy background. To couple with varied Q, the informative regions are selected to align full-precision models with Q via a masked attention alignment objective, thus yielding high-quality synthetic samples. Furthermore, a periodic sample refreshing strategy comes up to endow MaskAQ with the capacity to continually adapt to the evolving state of Q throughout the training process, to preserve desirable mutual information with synthetic samples. Extensive experiments verify the merits of MaskAQ over state-of-the-art approaches across multiple backbones and downstream tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/hfutqian/MaskAQ.

</details>


### [29] [Geometry-Preserving Unsupervised Alignment for Heterogeneous Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04385)
*Shuwen Yu,Zhanxuan Hu,Yi Zhao,Yonghang Tai,Huafeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GPUA是一个几何保持无监督对齐框架，通过正交映射将视觉基础模型的特征空间映射到视觉语言模型的语义空间，实现两者的互补优势整合，提高跨模型兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机视觉中的两大主导范式——视觉语言基础模型和视觉基础模型之间存在不完全兼容性。VLM提供语言语义对齐但视觉特征粗糙，VFM学习判别性感知几何但缺乏语义基础。需要整合两者的互补优势。

Method: 受跨语言对齐启发，GPUA将VFM特征视为"视觉语言"，学习一个正交映射，将VFM空间转换到VLM语义空间。该方法保持几何结构、缩小模态差距，无需标签或模型参数更新，任务无关且仅需特征级访问预训练模型。

Result: 在多样化基准测试上的实验表明，GPUA提高了跨模型兼容性，在下游零样本识别和分割任务中取得了显著性能提升，且计算开销可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: GPUA通过几何保持的无监督对齐有效整合了VFM和VLM的互补优势，为解决两种视觉基础模型范式之间的兼容性问题提供了一种高效、无需监督的解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation models have driven rapid progress in computer vision, yet the two dominant paradigms, vision-language foundation models (VLMs) and vision-only foundation models (VFMs), remain only partially compatible. VLMs offer language-grounded semantic alignment but are often visually coarse, while VFMs learn discriminative perceptual geometry but lack semantic grounding. We propose GPUA (Geometry-Preserving Unsupervised Alignment), a framework that integrates the complementary strengths of VFMs and VLMs. Inspired by cross-lingual alignment, GPUA treats VFM features as a visual language and learns an orthogonal mapping that translates the VFM space into the VLM semantic space, preserving geometry and narrowing the modality gap without labels or model parameter updates. GPUA is task-agnostic and requires only feature-level access to pretrained models. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved cross-model compatibility and strong gains in downstream zero-shot recognition and segmentation with negligible overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/Yuteam14/GPUA

</details>


### [30] [An Empirical Study of Data Scale, Model Complexity, and Input Modalities in Visual Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04409)
*Luoyidi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究分析了数据规模、模型复杂度和输入模态三个因素对深度神经网络视觉泛化性能的影响，发现增加训练数据规模始终能改善泛化性能，而模型复杂度变化则效果不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度神经网络参数规模大、结构非线性，在计算机视觉中表现出强大性能，但其泛化性能的来源难以用传统统计学习理论解释。数据规模、模型复杂度和输入模态是影响视觉泛化的基本可控因素，需要实证分析它们对模型泛化性能的具体影响。

Method: 研究包含初步实验和主要实验。初步实验构建一维非线性函数，通过改变训练样本数量和多项式阶数观察数据规模和模型复杂度的影响。主要实验在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，比较不同训练数据规模、模型架构和输入模态下的模型性能。输入模态包括彩色信息、梯度、边缘和小波等显式先验特征。

Result: 实验结果表明：1) 增加训练数据规模始终能改善泛化性能；2) 模型复杂度变化不能提供稳定的性能增益；3) 移除颜色信息会降低模型性能；4) 梯度、边缘和小波等显式先验特征在不同模型架构上的效果不一致。

Conclusion: 本研究对数据规模、模型复杂度、输入模态与视觉泛化性能之间的关系进行了实证分析，为理解深度神经网络的泛化机制提供了经验性证据，代码和实验记录已开源供进一步研究。

Abstract: Modern deep neural networks usually have large parameter scales and nonlinear hierarchical structures, and they have achieved strong performance in computer vision. However, the source of their generalization performance remains difficult to explain using traditional statistical learning theory. Among the factors that may affect visual generalization, data scale, model complexity, and input modalities are fundamental and controllable variables. This study empirically analyzes how these three factors influence model generalization performance. Specifically, in a preliminary experiment, we construct a one-dimensional nonlinear function and vary the number of training samples and the polynomial degree to observe the effects of data scale and model complexity on model performance. In the main experiments, we compare model performance on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 under different training data scales, model architectures, and input modalities. The experimental results show that increasing the training data scale consistently improves generalization performance, whereas changes in model complexity do not provide stable gains. In addition, removing color information degrades model performance, while explicit prior features such as gradients, edges, and wavelets have inconsistent effects across different model architectures. Overall, this study provides an empirical analysis of the relationships among data scale, model complexity, input modalities, and visual generalization performance. Code and experimental logs are available at: https://github.com/zlyd-CV/DeepLearning-Empirical-Studies.

</details>


### [31] [Ultra-Fast Neural Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04410)
*Jiahao Li,Wenxuan Xie,Zhaoyang Jia,Bin Li,Zongyu Guo,Xiaoyi Zhang,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCVC-UF是一种基于分块的超快神经视频编码框架，采用多帧分块编码为单一潜在表示，并结合跨帧交互模块和平行解码，显著提升了编码速度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经视频编码器虽然压缩比优越，但过高的计算复杂度阻碍了实际部署。现有方法通常逐帧处理，导致编码效率低下，需要新的范式来平衡率失真与计算复杂度。

Method: 提出分块编码框架，将多帧分块编码为单一紧凑潜在表示，采用跨帧交互模块进行联合时空建模，并行帧特定解码器进行同时重建，并设计了简化的熵编码机制减少比特流交互开销。

Result: DCVC-UF实现了超快的编码和解码速度，在性能上显著超越了以往的领先编解码器，在神经视频编码的率失真-复杂度权衡方面达到新的SOTA水平。

Conclusion: DCVC-UF通过分块编码框架、跨帧交互建模和简化熵编码的创新组合，成功解决了神经视频编码的计算瓶颈问题，标志着神经视频编码技术发展的重要里程碑。

Abstract: While neural video codecs (NVCs) have demonstrated superior compression ratio, their prohibitive computational complexity remains a critical barrier to real-world deployment. This paper introduces a chunk-based coding framework designed to significantly improve the rate-distortion-complexity trade-off. Instead of processing frames sequentially, our approach encodes a chunk of multiple frames into a single compact latent representation and decodes them simultaneously. This is enabled by cross-frame interaction modules for joint spatial-temporal modeling and frame-specific decoders for parallel reconstruction. This paradigm not only dramatically enhances coding throughput but also facilitates more effective modeling of long-term temporal correlations. To further boost speed, we propose a streamlined entropy coding mechanism that consolidates bit-stream interactions into a single step, substantially reducing decoding overhead. Building on these innovations, we present DCVC-UF (Ultra-Fast), a new NVC that sets a new SOTA in performance. Our experiments show that DCVC-UF can achieve ultra-fast encoding and decoding speeds, significantly outperforming previous leading codecs. DCVC-UF serves as a notable landmark in the journey of NVC evolution. The code is at https://github.com/microsoft/DCVC.

</details>


### [32] [Motion-Guided Causal Disentanglement for Robust Multi-View Cine Cardiac MRI Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04414)
*Chuankai Xu,Cristiane De Carvalho Singulane,Mohammad Abuannadi,Stephen Chandler,Jeremy Slivnick,Karolina Zareba,Jane Cao,Vidya Nadig,Fabio Fernandes,Seth Uretsky,Diego Perez de Arenaza,Amit Patel,Jianxin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Motion-Guided View-Disease Disentanglement (MoViD)框架，基于ViT-MAE主干网络，通过运动引导和多视图-疾病解耦来改善心脏磁共振图像分析，在疾病分类和分割任务中优于标准Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的心脏磁共振图像分析方法存在一个关键问题：它们学习统一的潜在嵌入，将视图特定的解剖变异与疾病相关特征纠缠在一起。这种纠缠导致分类器偏向结构属性而非视图不变的病理模式。在数据稀缺的情况下，特别是对于代表性不足的心脏疾病，这种问题更加严重，有限样本增加了对捷径学习和视图依赖决策边界的敏感性。

Method: 基于ViT-MAE主干网络构建MoViD框架：1）使用双分支监督对比目标显式分解潜在表示为视图特定和疾病判别成分；2）采用梯度反转对抗约束最小化疾病信息泄漏到视图嵌入；3）引入基于帧间差异图的无标注时间运动特征来定位心脏跳动区域并抑制背景伪影；4）在对比损失中加入焦点重加权机制缓解类别不平衡。

Result: 在私有的临床静脉血栓数据集和两个公共基准数据集（M&Ms, M&Ms2）上评估。在疾病分类和心脏分割任务中，该方法始终优于标准Transformer基线，并与大规模预训练基础模型竞争，验证了结构解耦在医学图像分析中的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的MoViD框架通过运动引导的视图-疾病解耦方法成功解决了心脏磁共振图像分析中视图特定变异与疾病特征纠缠的问题。该框架在数据稀缺情况下表现出优越性能，为医学图像分析中的结构解耦提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-view cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging provides complementary anatomical information and is widely used for noninvasive disease assessment. Recent transformer-based models have demonstrated strong representation learning capabilities for CMR analysis; however, they typically learn unified latent embeddings that entangle view-specific anatomical variations with disease-related features. Such entanglement biases classifiers toward structural attributes rather than view-invariant pathological patterns. This issue is exacerbated in low-data regimes, particularly for underrepresented cardiac conditions, where limited samples increase the susceptibility to shortcut learning and view-dependent decision boundaries. To address this, we propose a Motion-Guided View--Disease Disentanglement framework MoViD built upon a ViT-MAE backbone. The model explicitly factorizes latent representations into view-specific and disease-discriminative components using dual-branch supervised contrastive objectives and a gradient-reversal adversarial constraint that minimizes disease leakage into the view embedding. Additionally, an annotation-free temporal motion feature, derived from inter-frame difference maps, is introduced to localize the beating heart region and suppress background artifacts. A focal reweighting mechanism is incorporated into the contrastive loss to mitigate class imbalance. We evaluate the framework on a private clinical venous thrombosis dataset and two public benchmarks (M&Ms, M&Ms2). Across disease classification and cardiac segmentation tasks, our approach consistently outperforms standard transformer baselines and demonstrates competitive performance against large-scale pretrained foundation models, validating the efficacy of structural disentanglement in medical image analysis.

</details>


### [33] [Implicit Fuzzification via Bounded Noise Injection for Robust Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04427)
*Bisheng Tang,Zhangfeng Ma,Chuchu Zhai,Feng Dong,Yaoqun Wu,Ammar Oad,Yifei Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NoiseUNet通过在跳跃连接中注入有界扰动来正则化跨尺度特征融合，提高图像分割的边界模糊处理能力，并在甲状腺超声数据集ThyR上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像分割存在边界模糊问题，这源于采样引起的信息损失和像素级标记的内在不确定性。虽然U-Net等编码器-解码器架构性能良好，但通常产生过度自信的预测，无法捕捉过渡区域模糊性。

Method: 提出NoiseUNet框架，在跳跃连接中注入有界扰动来正则化跨尺度特征融合。这种机制强制模型对局部特征变化具有鲁棒性，并促进边界感知表示。理论上，扰动诱导了隐式模糊化效应，产生软的数据驱动隶属度，无需显式模糊建模。

Result: 实验表明NoiseUNet能一致地提高分割准确性和边界保真度。研究还引入了ThyR数据集，这是一个具有固有模糊边界的真实世界甲状腺超声数据集。

Conclusion: NoiseUNet是一个简单有效的框架，通过有界扰动正则化特征融合，解决了图像分割中的边界模糊问题，提高了模型的鲁棒性和边界感知能力。

Abstract: Image segmentation remains fundamentally limited by boundary ambiguity arising from sampling-induced information loss and inherent uncertainty in pixel-wise labeling. Although encoder-decoder architectures such as U-Net achieve strong performance, they often produce overconfident predictions that fail to capture transition-region ambiguity. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{NoiseUNet}, a simple yet effective framework that injects bounded perturbations into skip connections to regularize cross-scale feature fusion. This mechanism enforces robustness to local feature variations and promotes boundary-aware representations. Theoretically, the perturbation induces an implicit fuzzification effect, yielding soft, data-driven memberships without requiring explicit fuzzy modeling. We further introduce \textbf{ThyR}, a real-world thyroid ultrasound dataset with inherently ambiguous boundaries. Experiments demonstrate that NoiseUNet consistently improves both segmentation accuracy and boundary fidelity.

</details>


### [34] [DSA: Dynamic Step Allocation for Fast Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04432)
*Thanh-Tung Le,Yunhan Zhao,Menglei Chai,Zhengyang Shen,Zhe Cao,Danhang Tang,Xiaohui Xie,Deying Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DSA是一个面向自回归视频扩散模型的自适应计算框架，通过轻量级置信度头动态调整每帧去噪步数，在保持视频质量的同时实现实时生成（22.63 FPS）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散变压器虽然视觉质量领先，但推理成本高阻碍实时应用。近期蒸馏框架虽然降低了延迟，但仍采用固定去噪步数，导致计算资源浪费在简单帧上，而复杂帧却得不到充分细化。

Method: 1. 引入轻量级置信度头，与生成器联合训练基于分布匹配蒸馏目标；2. 置信度头估计每帧去噪可靠性；3. 推理时根据置信度信号动态调整扩散步数：简单帧提前终止（加速），复杂帧增加细化步数。

Result: 在H100 GPU上实现22.63 FPS的实时自回归视频生成，亚秒级延迟。在VBench评估中，与近期自回归和双向视频扩散模型相比，保持竞争性或更优的视频质量。

Conclusion: 置信度引导的自适应采样为实现交互式视频生成提供了有效且实用的途径，无需额外视频数据、无需启发式方法、仅需少量架构修改。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers have achieved state-of-the-art visual quality, but their high inference cost remains a major bottleneck for real-time applications. Recent distillation frameworks produce autoregressive video diffusion models with reduced latency, yet these models still use a fixed number of denoising steps per frame, wasting computation on predictable frames and under-refining challenging ones. We present DSA, a confidence-guided adaptive computation framework for AR video diffusion. DSA introduces a lightweight confidence head, trained jointly with the generator under a distribution-matching distillation objective, to estimate per-frame denoising reliability. At inference, this confidence signal dynamically adjusts the number of diffusion steps: simple frames terminate early for speed, while complex frames receive additional refinement. Our method requires no extra video data, no heuristics, and little architectural modification. Experiments show that DSA achieves real-time autoregressive video generation, reaching 22.63 FPS with sub-second latency on H100 GPUs, while maintaining competitive or superior VBench quality compared to recent autoregressive and bidirectional video diffusion models. Our results demonstrate that confidence-guided adaptive sampling provides an effective and practical path toward interactive video generation.

</details>


### [35] [Stateful Visual Encoders for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04433)
*Zirui Wang,Junwei Yu,Adam Yala,David M. Chan,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了状态视觉编码器，使视觉编码器具备记忆先前视觉特征的能力，在多图像多轮次交互中显著提升了视觉变化的检测和比较性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源视觉语言模型(VLMs)在多图像、多轮次的智能体应用中存在局限性：视觉编码器是无状态的，每张图像独立编码，无法访问先前的视觉上下文。这导致细微但任务关键的变化可能在语言模型有机会比较之前就被削弱，特别是当这些变化不影响场景的高层语义时。

Method: 引入状态视觉编码器，将每个视觉表示基于先前的视觉特征进行条件化。通过监督微调，为VLMs配备这种状态编码器，使其能够保留和利用跨图像的视觉信息。

Result: 状态编码器在跨图像空间聚合、多对象视觉差异检测和视觉轨迹行为克隆等控制任务上实现了持续改进。这些改进在不同输入分辨率、语言模型大小和VLM骨干网络中都保持一致。在纵向放射学、细粒度图像比较和遥感等实际任务中，状态编码器持续超越通用VLM基线，并在某些领域达到或超过了专用模型的性能。

Conclusion: 状态视觉编码器通过使视觉编码器具备记忆和利用先前视觉特征的能力，显著增强了VLMs在多图像、多轮次交互中对视觉变化的感知和比较能力，在各种视觉比较任务中提供了持续的性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in multi-image, multi-turn agentic settings where decisions depend on visual changes. However, in existing open-weight VLMs, visual comparisons happen only inside the language model, while the visual encoder itself remains stateless: each image is encoded independently, without access to the prior visual context. As a result, small but task-critical changes may be attenuated before the language model has a chance to compare them, especially when those changes do not affect the high-level semantics of the scene. We introduce a Stateful Visual Encoder, which conditions each visual representation on prior visual features. Under supervised finetuning, VLMs equipped with stateful encoders achieve consistent improvements on controlled tasks involving cross-image spatial aggregation, multi-object visual differencing, and visual trajectory behavior cloning. These improvements are consistent across input resolutions, language model sizes, and VLM backbones. Finally, we validate our model on real-world tasks, including longitudinal radiology, fine-grained image comparison, and remote sensing, where stateful encoders consistently improve generalist VLM baselines and can match or surpass specialized models in selected domains. Project page: https://statefulvisualencoders.github.io/

</details>


### [36] [Hyper-ICL: Attention Calibration with Hyperbolic Anchor Distillation for Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04434)
*Niloufar Alipour Talemi,Hossein Kashiani,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Hyper-ICL框架，通过参数高效的训练方法实现无需推理时演示的多模态上下文学习，提高准确性和稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态上下文学习存在高推理延迟和对演示格式、顺序及内容的敏感性导致的稳定性问题

Method: 学习参数高效的低秩logit级适配器校准注意力分布；引入查询自适应调制机制；设计层级双曲锚点蒸馏损失对齐中间特征

Result: 在六个多模态基准测试中（包括VQAv2、OK-VQA和COCO Caption），相比原始ICL和现有SOTA方法，一致提高了准确性和稳定性

Conclusion: Hyper-ICL成功解决了多模态上下文学习的延迟和稳定性问题，通过训练重建演示效果，无需推理时演示，实现了性能提升

Abstract: Multimodal In-Context Learning (ICL) has emerged as a practical inference paradigm for Multimodal Large Language Models, where a small set of interleaved image-text In-Context Demonstrations (ICDs) conditions the model to solve new tasks. Despite its flexibility, multimodal ICL incurs high inference latency and suffers from instability due to sensitivity to demonstration formatting, ordering, and content. To address these limitations, we propose Hyper-ICL, a lightweight, training-based framework for demonstration-free multimodal ICL that reconstructs demonstration effects directly without requiring ICDs at inference time. Hyper-ICL learns a parameter-efficient low-rank logit-level adapter that calibrates attention distributions to better match demonstration-induced attention redistribution. To capture how demonstration influence varies across queries, we introduce a query-adaptive modulation mechanism that adaptively controls intervention strength at token level across layers and heads based on the current query. Finally, we propose a layer-wise hyperbolic anchor distillation loss that aligns intermediate student features to a demonstration-conditioned teacher via Lorentz geodesic distance. This loss encourages the student to reconstruct the demonstration-query relationships induced by ICDs. Extensive experiments across six different multimodal benchmarks (including VQAv2, OK-VQA, and COCO Caption) demonstrate that Hyper-ICL consistently improves accuracy and stability over vanilla ICL and existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [37] [3DThinkVLA: Endowing Vision-Language-Action Models with Latent 3D Priors via 3D-Thinking-Guided Co-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04436)
*Jiaxin Shi,Xidong Zhang,Fucai Zhu,Zhe Li,Siyu Zhu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D思维引导的协同训练框架，使视觉-语言-动作模型在预测动作时能够隐式进行3D空间推理，无需3D传感器或显式文本生成，保持2D图像输入的简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型在3D空间推理方面存在不足，需要3D传感器或显式文本推理链。需要开发能够在2D图像输入下隐式进行3D空间推理的方法，同时避免灾难性遗忘。

Method: 基于3D思维引导的协同训练框架，包含三个组件：1) 潜在3D几何感知模块，将视觉特征与3D基础模型对齐获取几何先验；2) 在线3D推理蒸馏模块，通过共享推理锚标记传输空间推理能力；3) 空间增强动作集成，将解耦的几何和推理特征注入动作查询标记中。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-PLUS、SimplerEnv和真实世界操作任务上达到最先进性能，无需3D传感器、外部模型或显式文本生成，同时防止预训练VLM的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将3D几何感知和空间推理解耦到不同特征层次，通过协同训练框架实现了隐式3D推理，为基于2D图像的VLA模型提供了有效的3D空间理解能力。

Abstract: We propose a 3D-thinking-guided co-training framework that enables vision-language-action (VLA) models to perform 3D spatial reasoning implicitly during action prediction. Our core insight is that 3D geometry perception and 3D spatial reasoning are distinct capabilities that can be disentangled and injected at different feature hierarchies.
